# Architecture

**Version:** V3.325 (Protocol importer + `/protocol-import` skill catch-up — both now understand every block kind from FuturePlan 03 Releases 1–4, closing FuturePlan 03. `rm/js/protocol-import.js` `validate` gains per-kind checks (callout type / calculator output name+expr / builder dataset+dims / layoutMap dataset / unknown-kind warning) backed by new `KNOWN_KINDS` + `CALLOUT_TYPES` constants mirroring ProtocolSchemas; `summarize` counts all new kinds and the import preview in `rm/js/protocols.js` surfaces the widget counts when present. `.claude/skills/protocol-import/SKILL.md` documents the full widget set (compact JSON examples + attrs per kind), the inline vocabulary grammar, formula-vs-calculator-vs-builder selection guidance, and an expanded validation checklist; guidance now says emit `callout` not legacy note/safety. FTP-only (importer + protocols.js + skill doc); no Firestore changes. `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 295 → 297 (296 shipped the feature, 297 folds in adversarial-review fixes: an align-attribute XSS in the doc-view calculator table header, a Firestore `undefined`-save break on cleared decimals, builder→layoutMap/computed-table live refresh in doc view, and a silent plate-overflow warning). Plan: `CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.325_protocol_import_catchup.md`. **FuturePlan 03 complete** — the Protocols Builder now has document chrome (V3.315), rich tables/kv/timeline (V3.316), calculators (V3.318), the builder+plate-map design panel (V3.324), and importer parity (V3.325).) Previously V3.324 (Protocols Builder interactive builder + plate map — FuturePlan 03 Release 4, completing the gold-standard design panel with Release 3. New `builder` block kind (`rm/js/protocol-schemas.js` `newBuilder`): `attrs.dataset` output name; `attrs.params[{name,label,min,max,step,default,unit,lt}]` — sliders published to the shared symbol table via new `builderToFormulaEntries` in `rm/js/protocol-formulas.js` (wired into `collectFormulas` + the doc view's plain walker), `lt` names another param this one is clamped strictly below (monotonic Lₛ<L_M<L_L chains); `attrs.dims[{field,label,source:'params'|'list',options,editable}]` segmented pickers (params dims stamp field/field_label/field_value on rows; editable list dims = session-editable ratio editor); `attrs.derived[{field,expr}]` per-row computed fields; `attrs.dedupe{when,ignore[]}` — truthy-`when` rows get `ignore` fields blanked before identity-keying so duplicates merge (f=1 inert-merge). Doc-view panel (`renderBuilder` in `rm/js/protocol-doc.js`): sliders + pickers + Add / Add-all (cartesian) / remove / Clear; assembly session-local, re-registered via `registerDataset` on every change so calculator computed tables + layout maps refresh live; param-bound row values and derived fields re-sync as sliders move, fingerprint-guarded against the protocol-vars-changed feedback loop. New `layoutMap` block kind: dataset → plate grid (`rows`/`cols` default 4×6, `cellLabel`/`cellSub` row exprs, `colorField` → 8-class palette, spare wells auto-filled, A/B/C row + numeric column headers). Editor renderers are config surfaces; the calculator's array-grid editor hoisted to module-level `_arrayGridEditor` (+ csv field kind) and shared. Doc view loads mathjs whenever builders/layout maps are present. "+ add block" menu gains builder/layoutMap. No Firestore changes. `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 294 → 295. Plan: `CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.324_protocol_builder_layoutmap.md`.) Previously V3.318 (Protocols Builder calculator — FuturePlan 03 Release 3. New `calculator` block kind (`rm/js/protocol-schemas.js` `newCalculator`): `attrs.inputs[{name,label,widget:'slider'|'number',min,max,step,unit,rangeHint,default}]`, `attrs.outputs[{name,label,expr,unit,decimals}]` (hero cards), `attrs.table{dataset,columns[{label,expr,align,mono,decimals}]}` — all plain JSON on the attrs Y.Map. Engine (`rm/js/protocol-formulas.js`): `collectFormulas` + the doc view's plain walker now visit calculator blocks via shared `calculatorToFormulaEntries(block)` (inputs → variables, each output → expression entry) so `buildSymbolTable` is reused unchanged and calculator values land in the same symbol table as formula blocks; new session-scoped named-datasets registry `registerDataset/getDataset/listDatasets` (re-emits `protocol-vars-changed` on register; Release-4 builder is the producer) and `evalExpression(expr, table, math, extraScope?)` evaluating under the numeric scope with per-row fields shadowing globals. Editor renderer (`renderCalculator` in `rm/js/protocol-blocks.js`) is config grids + live outputs preview; the doc view (`rm/js/protocol-doc.js`) renders slider/number control rows writing session-local overrides, hero-card grids, and computed tables evaluating each column expr per dataset row. "+ add block" menu gains calculator. No Firestore changes. `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 288 → 289. Plan: `CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.318_protocol_calculator.md`.) Previously V3.316 (Protocols Builder rich tables + definition list + timeline — FuturePlan 03 Release 2. Three data-display block kinds added to `rm/js/protocol-schemas.js` (`newTable`/`newKv`/`newTimeline` + normalize defaults): `table` (`attrs.columns[{key,label,align,mono}]` + `rows[{key:val}]` — editor has per-column alignment cycling, monospace toggle, add/remove col+row; doc view routes every cell through `renderProse` so var chips / pills / sub-sup work inside cells; optional body caption), `kv` (definition/derivation list `attrs.items[{term,def}]`, doc view renders a two-column `<dl>` with mono terms), and `timeline` (`attrs.marks[{at,label,sub,included}]`, doc view renders dots on a horizontal rail with mono tick captions; `included:false` marks render dimmed+struck — the gold standard's not-run condition points; optional body track title). All three store attrs as PLAIN JSON arrays on the attrs Y.Map (coarse-grained collaboration, safe post-V3.315 Array.isArray dehydrate fix); editor renderers re-render on remote attr changes only when the block doesn't hold focus. Decision: `materials` stays its own kind rather than becoming a generic-table preset — its inventory row-linking (Y.Array rows, item_ref chips, V3.139 matcher) is deeply Y-typed and rewiring it risks that machinery for zero user-visible gain. "+ add block" menu gains table/kv/timeline. No Firestore changes. `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 286 → 287. Plan: `CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.316_protocol_tables_kv_timeline.md`.) Previously V3.315 (Protocols Builder document chrome — FuturePlan 03 Release 1. Three new block kinds in `rm/js/protocol-schemas.js` (`PROTOCOL_BLOCK_KINDS` + factories + `normalizeTree` defaults): `heading` (stage code + title + optional rule), `callout` (`attrs.type ∈ critical|note|assume|timing` — folds the legacy `note`/`safety` kinds at RENDER level: the dispatcher in `rm/js/protocol-blocks.js` routes all three kinds through `renderCallout` with a type mapping (note→note, safety→critical) and stored kinds are untouched until the user changes the type select, which rewrites that block to `kind:'callout'` in one transaction), and `docHeader` (masthead: `{brand, title, chips:[{text,var}]}` where chips is a plain JSON array on the attrs Y.Map — `dehydrateBlock` gained an `Array.isArray` branch because the generic `forEach` case was coercing plain arrays to `{0:…}` objects). Inline vocabulary: `ProtocolFormulas.renderProse(raw, table?)` is now the single prose renderer — one sorted-by-offset pass over `[!var:]` + `[!inv:]` + new `[!pill:kind:text]` tokens (functional/inert get dedicated colors) plus `` `code` ``/`~sub~`/`^sup^` inline markup applied to the plain segments; `bindYTextWithVars`'s private two-token merge deleted in favor of it, `textHasProse` is the new skip-fast gate. `buildSymbolTable(formulas, math, overrides?)` accepts a session-local overrides map ({name→number} shadowing variable defaults, never persisted) with module-level `setOverride/getOverrides/clearOverrides` + `_lastYTree` re-rebuild — powers the doc view's live inputs today and Release-3 sliders later. Read-only document view: `pages/protocols.html?protocol=X&view=doc` is a third page mode (`data-protocol-mode="doc"`) loading `rm/js/protocol-doc.js` (new, ~450 lines) + `rm/css/protocol-doc.css` (new) — renders the PROJECTED JSON tree (one Firestore doc read, zero Yjs) with branded masthead (first `docHeader` block anywhere becomes the masthead; brand falls back to `labConfig/branding` best-effort then "McGhee Lab"), numbered stage headers from section labels (`01 ·` style), continuous step numbering, color-coded callouts (timing renders as the gold standard's pill), materials tables with inventory-resolved labels, figures, and live calculator cards per formula block (number inputs recompute the shared symbol table session-locally through a `refreshers[]` closure bus; prose var chips + masthead chips + outputs all refresh). `@media print` hides shell chrome so browser print-to-PDF is the export; toolbar `🖨 Print / PDF` button. Editor topbar gains a `📄 Doc view` link; "+ add block" menu now offers callout/heading/docHeader instead of note/safety. Grep confirmed no consumer outside the protocol modules reads raw `kind:'note'|'safety'`. No Firestore rules/index changes — all new state lives inside the existing `tree`. `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 285 → 286. Plan: `CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.315_protocol_doc_chrome.md`.) The Firestore team overlay's Pass 3 gains an alumni branch: members grouped by `priorCategory` in order postdoc → grad ("PhD") → masters → undergrad → highschool → unknown "Alumni" bucket, each non-empty group rendered as `.alumni-subheader` + own `.team-grid`/detail panel via `wireTeamDetailPanel`. Previously V3.311 (Missing-PDF queue — `rm/js/library.js` gains `_pdfStatus` (ok|missing|unobtainable via `meta.library.pdf_status`), a "Missing PDFs (N)" toolbar filter chip, and per-row ↻ Retry (Unpaywall via LIBRARY_UPLOAD exports, stamps `pdf_retry{attempts,last_at,last_error}` + `pdf_candidate_url` on CORS block) / ⬆ Upload (attaches to the SAME item id, then ref-parse + SS enrich) / 🚫↩ unobtainable toggle, all persisted through a new targeted `_saveOne` single-doc write. `rm/js/library-paper.js` `_renderNoPdfActions` turns the reader's no-PDF dead end into the same queue actions with candidate-URL link.) New `rm/js/library-refresh.js` `fullRefresh` (lookup incl. title-search fallback → targeted set → Unpaywall OA attempt via newly-exported `LIBRARY_UPLOAD.unpaywallLookup/fetchPdfBlob` → SS re-enrich → ref parse) with ↻ Refresh from DOI in the reader sidebar.) Previously V3.309 (Library metadata editor — new `rm/js/library-edit.js` modal (`LIBRARY_EDIT.open`): edit all light metadata fields + abstract (extras subdoc via `LIBRARY_EXTRAS`), fetch-from-identifier per-field diff (blank fields pre-checked), targeted `items/{id}.set()` with serverTimestamp `updatedAt` (cache-probe friendly), opt-in citation-key regeneration. Entry points: library row ✎ + "⚠ Fix metadata" chip (`library.js`), paper-reader sidebar ✎ (`library-paper.js`, which now also loads `library-metadata.js`). firestore.rules `items` update gains a lab-member clause scoped to `meta.library.is_library_entry==true` with `first_author_uids` pinned via `.get(...,[])` and `type`/`is_library_entry` immutable — also un-breaks member star-toggles.) Previously V3.308 (Production library metadata lookup — new Cloud Function `libraryLookup` (`functions/library-lookup.js`): CORS + Bearer-token proxy porting server.py's CrossRef/PubMed/arXiv/title lookups 1:1 (byte-compatible with `buildPaperItem`), gated to non-guest users, with a 30-day Firestore `metadataCache/{sha1}` collection (deny-all client rules; Admin SDK only). `rm/js/library-metadata.js` branches transport on `RM_RUNTIME.isLocal` (dev server first with Cloud Function fall-through; prod straight to the function) and moves first-page extraction client-side via `PDF_VIEWER.extractTextItemsFromPdf` (new `opts.maxPages`), adding a largest-font `title_guess`. `rm/js/library.js` title-search fallbacks (ingest + add-by-identifier) now confirm fuzzy matches before applying. Makes add-by-DOI / ingest sniffing / capture enrichment work on the static deploy for the first time.) Previously V3.304 (External teacher & student platform — outside teachers request a class, admin approves and provisions a teacher-owned `schedules` doc (`kind:'teacher-class'`), students join with a 6-char code. New Firestore collections `classRequests` / `joinCodes` (doc id IS the code) / `enrollments` (doc id `${scheduleId}_{uid}`, create rules-gated on a live join code — no Cloud Function) / `reviews` (approved-only public reads) / `teacherProfiles` (admin-only `approved` status) with rules + two composite indexes. Previously V3.300 (New standalone course `modules/surface_functionalization/` — four interactive Surface Functionalization Foundations lessons (surface energy & adsorbed water → plasma treatment → silanes → roughness & superhydrophobicity) built per `modules/AUTHORING.md`, each escalating high-school → sophomore-undergraduate across four canvas-sim tabs. L1: missing-neighbor imbalance, γ ≈ Nε/2A, BET multilayer water adsorption, vacuum kinetics (mean free path, Hertz–Knudsen monolayer time). L2: pump-down electron runways, the Paschen breakdown curve, gas-specific plasma emission colors, gas→group grafting (O₂→OH, N₂→NH₂, CO₂→COO⁻). L3: silane head condensation onto surface –OH, the trace-water Goldilocks hydrolysis/condensation window, tail-set surface energy, Young’s equation. L4: Wenzel amplification, Cassie–Baxter trapped air (lotus effect), the Cassie↔Wenzel stability criterion + irreversible impalement collapse, Sharklet topographic anti-fouling. Reuses the solid_mechanics helper-library contract (COL/setupCanvas/Chart/glyphs/setStat/setVerdict) verbatim and the TFM startAnim/stopAnim teardown for animated tabs. overview.html landing page on the solid_mechanics/overview.html pattern; four printable predict→do→record→explain worksheets. Previously V3.296 (New standalone course `modules/solid_mechanics/` — five interactive Solid Mechanics Foundations lessons (atoms-as-springs → grains → force/displacement → stress/strain → loading modes) built per `modules/AUTHORING.md`; fully self-contained (MathJax CDN only, no SPA header/Firebase), registered in `modules/manifest.json` under a new `solid_mechanics` folder. Shared `setupCanvas` helper hardened against a devicePixelRatio feedback loop that shrank canvases to an error on Retina displays (intrinsic size now cached in dataset, not re-read from the reflected width attribute). Previously V3.295 (Paper reader two-column gutter detection. rm/js/pdf-viewer.js reading-order builder no longer leaks highlights across a two-column gutter. `_clusterAndSort(items, pxInk)` is a dispatcher: `_detectColumnGutters` finds vertical whitespace channels (x-occupancy projection with a valley-FLOOR-anchored ceiling so full-width title/abstract/heading content cannot fill the valley in and hide it — the main cause of papers still not recognized; per-row occupancy clean-test + 2-pass median smoothing + consistency guard against typographic rivers), then `_orderByColumns` orders column-major within horizontal bands (robust to any bridge — narrow gutter or full-width element — since order is geometry-derived, not flood-fill connectivity). No-gutter pages keep the original DBSCAN via `_orderByFloodFill` (single-column/figure behaviour unchanged). `_makePixelProbe` downsamples the rendered canvas into an ink grid so the clean-test also catches non-text content (figures/equations/images) spanning the gutter, ignoring thin column rules via a both-edges check. The {index,cluster} item contract + getTextItems() API are unchanged (geom-selection/paper-citations/paper-search/paper-overlay-import unaffected). Pure helpers are module-scope + unit-tested. Previously V3.294 (Paper reader tabs. `pages/library-paper.html` keeps up to 10 papers open at once via a `.lp-tabs#lp-tabs` strip; switching is instant in-place (no reload/re-render, per-tab scroll+zoom preserved). `#lp-viewer` is now the host `#lp-viewer-host` holding one `.lp-viewer-pane` per *visited* tab — each owns its own `PDF_VIEWER` instance, created lazily on first activation; only the active pane is shown and it carries `id="lp-viewer"` so existing lookups resolve to it. `library-paper.js` was refactored from a one-shot `init()` into a tab manager: `bootOnce()` (one-time wiring + items load + persisted-tab reconcile), `activateTab(id)`/`closeTab(id)`, `_ensurePaneActive` (create-or-show pane+viewer), and `_detachActive`↔`_attachActive` which tear down and re-init the shared annotation/overlay/citation/search singleton stack on every switch. Open papers persist at `localStorage: rm_lp_open_papers`; the 10-cap evicts the least-recently-active tab. Every existing reader entry point (library/citations/cited-by/PMR/grants/sharing → `library-paper.html?id=`) now lands as a focused/added tab. Teardown hardening so re-init is leak-free: `paper-overlays.js` + `paper-search.js` bind their outside-click closers once via named handlers removed in `destroy()`; `paper-overlay-import.js` re-targets its drag/drop listeners to the active pane (`init({viewerEl})` + `destroy()`); `paper-overlay-types.js` resets `_changeCallbacks` in `init()` + adds `destroy()`; `annotation-panel.js` `init()` resets `_filterCallbacks` + per-card maps; `pdf-viewer.js` `destroy()` removes its container scroll listener; the orchestrator binds `copy` once in `bootOnce` and guards the `setZoom` overlay-redraw wrap per-viewer. Client-only deploy. Previously V3.293 (Classifier Settings & Transparency page. New `rm/pages/classifier-settings.html` + `rm/js/classifier-settings.js` (lab-member gated; linked from nav `Settings → Classifier Settings` and the API-page card) exposes every classifier knob and explains decisions. `email-classifier.js` gains `configure(patch)`/`getConfig()` — tunable `minExamples`/`minPerClass`/`minConfidence`/`alpha` + per-feature-group `weights{from,dom,s,b}` (defaults reproduce prior behavior) — and `explain(email)` returning ranked probabilities + per-class per-feature contribution; scoring refactored into one weighted `_classLogScore()` shared by `_scores`/`explain`. Settings persist to `userData/{uid}/emailArchive/classifierSettings` (route `email_archive/classifier_settings.json`) and `email-review.js` `boot()` applies them via `loadClassifierSettings()` before training. `category-rules.js` exposes `describe()` (rule tables read-only) for the transparency view. Page shows: live resubstitution accuracy (overall + per-class, recomputed as weights change), feature-weight + threshold sliders, a per-email "explain" (probability bars + which category each feature favors + per-category training counts), and the deterministic fallback-rule tables. Client-only deploy. Previously V3.292 (Live-learning email category classifier. New `rm/js/email-classifier.js` (`window.EMAIL_CLASSIFIER`) is a self-contained in-browser Multinomial Naive Bayes over features `from:<sender>` / `dom:<domain>` / `s:<subject token>` / `b:<snippet token>`, trained from a labeled store at `userData/{uid}/emailArchive/classifierTraining` (`email_archive/classifier_training.json` route; one doc `{examples:{id:{label,sub,s,subj,snip,ts}}}`). `email-review.js` `guessCategory()` is the single source of truth for auto-guesses: learned model (when `ready()` ≥12 examples & confidence ≥0.6) → `CATEGORY_RULES` → unknown; used in `_coerceLegacyEmailShape` at load and in `_recheckGuesses()` which re-predicts every non-manual row. Each hand-categorization (`quickSetCategory`, full-picker `onChange`, `bulkReassignCategory`) calls `recordTrainingExample` (upsert + retrain + persist) then `_recheckGuesses()` so a correction retrains the model and propagates to all other guesses immediately; "Clear override" calls `removeTrainingExample`. `boot()` trains before loading year data + backfills pre-existing manual overrides. Model-sourced guesses render a green "learned" badge; quick-sort pills rank via the model when ready. API Usage page (`api-usage.html`) gains a top "Email category classifier — training data" card (`rm/js/classifier-review.js`, IIFE) showing example count / active-or-warming status / optimistic training accuracy / vocabulary / per-category counts / most-predictive signals (`topTokens` log-odds) / the full training set with delete — all client-side (the suggester cards below remain local-Python-dev-only). No LLM/API key/Cloud Function; the `userData/{uid}/{document=**}` rule already covers the new doc. Client-only deploy. Previously V3.291 (Email date + display-cap fixes. The Gmail scraper stores `date` as a raw RFC-2822 header string but the renderer/timeline/sort assumed ISO — `_coerceLegacyEmailShape` now normalizes `date` to ISO from `internalDate` (epoch ms; fallback parse header), fixing the empty Monthly-volume chart (`date.slice(0,7)` month bucket), the date column, and chronological order. The date sort uses a numeric `emailTime(e)` helper (`internalDate` → `Date.parse`) instead of `localeCompare`. `EMAIL_PAGE_LIMIT` 250→2000 + `renderEmails` cap 500→1000 so a ~1000-msg backfill isn't truncated to ~150 visible. `renderActivityRollup` falls back to category when `activity_type` is absent so "Top activities" isn't empty for un-sub-categorized scraped mail. Client-only; FTP + reload. Previously V3.290 (Email triage usability pass on top of the V3.289 rules pre-filter. (1) Inline one-click quick-sort: every uncategorized row carries an always-open "Sort into:" pill bar — `renderQuickCategorize(e)` ranks candidates via new `CATEGORY_RULES.rank(email)` (★-marked best guess + remaining real categories), one click calls `quickSetCategory(e, cat)` to write a category-only manual override; "more…" opens the full picker. (2) "Days back" backfill: `#email-scrape-days` input drives manual scrapes only — `scrape-now.js` `scrapeGmail(db,uid,days)` uses `newer_than:${days}d`, paginates `nextPageToken` up to 2000 msgs, and bypasses the 60-s rate limit; the background poll stays incremental. (3) Uncategorized rows flagged with a red inset left tab + dot. (4) ✨ disposition button no longer hits the local-only `/api/refine-disposition` (404 in prod) — `guessDisposition(e)` computes actionable/reminder/info client-side from category+subject. `category-rules.js` scoring extracted into shared `scoreEmail()`; `rank()` returns confidence-sorted candidates, `[]` when no rule matches. Redeploy `firebase deploy --only functions:scrapeNow`. Previously V3.289 (Email rules pre-filter — scraped mail auto-sorts into real categories at render time via a free, client-side JS port of the lab's Python rules pre-pass. New `rm/js/category-rules.js` exposes `window.CATEGORY_RULES.guess(email)` / `classifyNoise(subject,sender)` — a 1:1 port of `ResearchManagement/scripts/email_classify_rules.py` `classify_one` (DOMAIN_HINTS over `from[].email` + SUBJECT_HINTS, summed-weight top category, `needs_llm = conf<0.6` + tie-break) and `email_noise_rules.py` `classify_noise` (definite-noise subject/sender patterns → category 'noise'). `email-review.js` `_coerceLegacyEmailShape` runs it for any mail lacking a backfilled/manual category and stamps `category_source:'rule'` (falls back to 'unknown' when no rule matches); `applyOverrideToEmail` still wins for manual overrides; rows show a tentative "guess" badge for rule-sourced categories beside the "edit" badge for manual. No LLM / API key / Cloud Function / Firestore write — runs in the browser, free. The Haiku 4.5 few-shot LLM guesser (deployed onCall + onDocumentCreated auto-on-scrape + one-time Batch backlog, "learns as you correct" via `category_overrides` few-shot, no retraining) is the documented next step — the entire ML/LLM categorizer today is local-Python-only in the sibling ResearchManagement repo and never runs in production.) Previously V3.288 (Always-fresh email scraping — Email Review (`?tab=email` on `activity-overview.html`) stays current without a button press. Three layers: (1) server `scrapeGmailForAllUsers` runs every 10 min (was 15) for all connected users even with no page open; (2) on load the tab fires an immediate background sync then polls every 10 min while visible+online via `_emailStartPoll`/`_emailStopPoll` (gated on `document.visibilityState==='visible'` + `navigator.onLine`, with throttled `visibilitychange`/`online`/`pagehide` catch-up); (3) the manual "Scrape now" button stays but is hardened. `onScrapeEmailsNow({quiet})` is one path for manual+background — `navigator.onLine` guard FIRST (the offline silent-failure that prompted this), a `TypeError`/`Failed to fetch` network-error branch, an `_emailScrape` re-entrancy/throttle coordinator — wired via a delegated `document` click listener registered at module-eval time so it survives a `boot()` bail. First-time/disconnected users get an inline Connect-Gmail card (`_renderConnectGmailEmptyState`/`_connectGmailInline`) that runs the OAuth popup via the new reusable `OAUTH_CONNECTIONS.connectGoogle({onDone,onError})` (extracted from the Settings card's private `connect()`); the email tab now also loads `gsi/client` + `oauth-connections.js`. `_isGmailConnected()` reads `userData/{uid}/private/gmailCredential.refreshTokenStored`; `loadSummary()` now treats an empty mailbox as a valid empty paged summary instead of throwing into the legacy on-disk dev path. Root cause superseded: the manual-scrape feature was uncommitted/undeployed, so prod served handler-less JS and the button silently did nothing.) Previously V3.287 (Lab Foundations overview module — beginner entry point `modules/foundations/overview.html` for a series of topic-organized, lab-based learning tracks. First live track Hydrogel Mechanics links the four TFM modules (hydrogels & cell forces → network formation → ball indentation → traction force microscopy); Microscopy Basics / Cell Culture & Sample Prep / Data Analysis & Modeling are scaffolded as "coming soon". Registered under a new `foundations` folder in `modules/manifest.json` so it's pickable in the class-builder module picker. Class-aware track links resolve `classId` from the page's `?class=` param, else from the parent SPA hash (`#/classes/{id}/...`) when inside the sandboxed module-viewer iframe — in-iframe links open in a new tab carrying `?class=` since top-navigation is sandboxed off, standalone links navigate in place. Shipped as a learning module attached to an existing Firestore class; no SPA router/nav/`content.json` changes.) Previously V3.286 (BME 295C Course Assessment tab — end-of-course feedback form for students, aggregated admin panels for the next-cohort retrospective. Per-student doc at `schedules/bme295c/assessments/{auth.uid}` with `ratings/picks/texts/perSpeaker/anonymous/status/campusAtWrite/rosterIdAtWrite/gateStateAtWrite/lastEditedBy/lastEditedName/lastEditedAt/appVersion` shape. Hardcoded question catalog `BME_ASSESS_COURSE_QS` (4 Likert/pick + 4 free-text course-level + per-speaker stars+comment per speaker, grouped by day). Save path inherits V3.285 hardening: `showDropReason` visible early-return banners, in-flight guard (`_bmeAssessmentSubmitInFlight`), autosave `clearTimeout` to kill race-clobbers, 30s safety-net + `.finally(release)`. Admin gates via new `assessmentState: 'closed' | 'open' | 'locked'` field (default `closed`, splice'd through `persistAll` trap + schedule onSnapshot); flipped via `setBmeAssessmentState`. Four new admin dashboard panels stack between Discussion Gates and Live Monitor: gate + summary + CSV export buttons; course-level histograms (text-bar bars per question with mean + bucket counts); open-ended responses (grouped `<details>` per text question with name/anonymous byline + tooltip resolving to real name); per-speaker results (mean stars + recent 3 comments + `<details>` for rest, grouped by day). Admin reads via new `_bmeDashboardData.assessments` Map populated by `loadBmeDashboardData` + kept live by new `attachBmeAssessmentsListener` / `detachBmeAssessmentsListener` (twin of V3.251 dashboard listener). Two CSV exports via `bmeAssessmentsNumericCSV` + `bmeAssessmentsTextCSV` + `bmeDownloadAssessmentsCSV` (RFC 4180 escapes, test-mode rows excluded). Firestore rules add `match /assessments/{uid}` inside `match /schedules/{scheduleId}` — stricter than `groupResponses` (which is public-read) because free-text feedback is sensitive: read self OR `isAdmin()` OR class admin via `classAdminEmails`; write self only with paranoid `lastEditedBy == auth.uid` check; delete admin only. Tab + section registered via `SECTION_REG.assessment295c`, `DEFAULT_SCHEDULES['bme295c'].tabs` entry, `wireClassPage` migration block (clones seed tab into existing schedule docs that lack it), `renderSectionBody` switch case, `loadBme295cData` per-user prefetch. Honor-system anonymity: docId is student's uid (so edit-your-own works) and `anonymous` flag drives the admin display; admin tooltip still resolves the real name via `lastEditedName` for follow-up. `_adminViewOverride` guard in the save path prevents an admin in V3.248 view-as-student mode from writing a feedback doc under their own uid.) — V3.285 (BME 295C submit reliability hardening — 5 fixes from the Workflow audit (`wf_622dff3f-796`) that diagnosed "some groups couldn't submit". Verdict: 14 code bugs, 2 rare student-side causes. (Fix 1) `saveBmeGroupResponsePrompts` early-return preconditions are now visible — each drop reason writes to `statusEl` AND adds `.cb295-response-submit-failed` to the submit button for a 6s red flash. Pre-V3.285 five silent-drop paths showed zero UI feedback. (Fix 2) Legacy `entry.groupNumber` fallback in `bmeGetGroupForDay` DROPPED — was routing every Tue-Fri save to Monday's docId whenever admin hadn't auto-assigned past day 1; new `.cb295-response-no-group-strip` callout fires instead so the student knows the admin needs to run auto-assign. (Fix 3) Submit handler triple race-condition fix: `_bmeSubmitInFlight` Set dedups duplicate clicks; `clearTimeout(_bmeResponseSaveTimers[slug])` kills the pending 800ms autosave so it can't race the submit's `merge:true` write and clobber `status: 'submitted'` back to `'draft'`; new `onCancel` callback on `openBmeAttendanceModal` releases the button when the modal is dismissed without confirming. (Fix 4) New `attachBmeScheduleListener` / `detachBmeScheduleListener` + `_bmeScheduleUnsub` — student-side onSnapshot on the schedule doc itself, parallel to the V3.251 `groupResponses` listener. Splices whitelisted admin-mutable fields (`discussionState`, `dailyAttendance`, `studentRoster`, `instructors`, `classAdminEmails`, `testMode`, `speakerVideos`) into the live `_classData` and re-runs the email→roster lookup so `_bmeRosterEntry` picks up new daily-group assignments without a reload. Excluded for admin sessions to prevent stutter. New `flushPendingResponseAutosaves` runs before the splice + `refreshCanvas` — captures live textarea values into `_bmeGroupResponses` and defers the actual Firestore write ~50ms so the rebuild rehydrates with in-flight keystrokes instead of stale cache (closes the data-loss race the V3.285 verification workflow `wf_59c667e1-689` flagged before ship). (Fix 5) Gate-state check at the top of `saveBmeGroupResponsePrompts` blocks writes past closed discussions even if cache is stale; `gateStateAtWrite` payload field stamps the gate state on every doc for forensic audit. Combined effect: submits are visible, gate flips reach students within seconds, races can't clobber status, and any future regression of these paths leaves a Firestore audit trail.) — V3.284 (BME 295C group response routing fix. Two production-breaking docId mismatches found by the workflow audit are repaired: (1) `bmeSpeakerDay` now strips the date suffix so the `"Monday, June 8"` form from content.json normalizes to the bare weekday key that `dailyGroups` uses — pre-fix, every `bmeGetGroupForDay` returned null and the save path silently bailed; (2) new `bmeStudentCampus(entry)` wraps `bmeEffectiveCampus(entry, bmeProfileFor(entry))` and replaces raw `student.campus` at every admin docId construction site (`bmeStudentGotDiscussionCredit`, `bmeComputeStudentGrade` perTalk rows, `bmeLiveMonitorInner` `campusGroups`, `renderBmeGradeDiscussionsPanel` `collectGroups`) — pre-fix, save wrote to `effective-N-slug` but the live monitor + gradebook read from `roster-N-slug`, so any campus-override student's response was invisible to admin. Defensive `console.warn` added at `bmeResponseDocId`, `bmeSpeakerDay`, `saveBmeGroupResponsePrompts` (per-field branches instead of a single short-circuit), and a `loadBme295cData` integrity loop — so any future regression of these mismatches surfaces in DevTools instead of silently dropping. Per the user's "ensure any already submitted groups are not deleted" constraint, no destructive migration ships — instead `renderBmeOrphanedDraftsBlock` surfaces under the live monitor: every Firestore doc whose id doesn't match the post-fix expected `(campus × group × slug)` triple for any roster student gets a row with **View** (opens the read-only response modal) + **Copy text** (clipboard the 3 prompt bodies for paste-recovery). Slotted inside `renderBmeLiveMonitorPanel`; `rerenderBmeLiveMonitor` keeps both halves in lock-step on live updates.) — V3.283 (Dedicated Grade discussions by group admin panel. `renderBmeGradeDiscussionsPanel` renders one `<details>` per speaker, one row per (campus, group) combo, with status pill + word count + attendance ratio + 1-3 star picker + View. Writes through the same `bmeSetDiscussionQuality` writer the V3.280 per-student detail modal uses, so the two surfaces stay in sync. Stars apply to ALL students in the group via the existing composite math (`bmeComputeStudentGrade` multiplies discussion credit by `stars/3` for every member). Slotted in `renderBmeAdminDashBody` between Gradebook and Discussion Gates.) — V3.282 (Discussion prompts hidden from students until admin opens the gate. `renderBmeModulesBody` per-speaker template now branches on `gateState === 'pending' && _viewType !== 'admin'`: that branch renders a `.cb295-mod-pending-callout` in place of the prompts + response shell. Admin viewers always see the prompts with a small "admin preview — pending" tag so they can review content while still gated. Open and locked states unchanged. View-as → Student in the V3.248 toolbar lets admin verify the student-side hidden view.) — V3.281 (Alertness moved from per-talk to per-day + integrated into the Daily Attendance panel. `grades.alertness` storage shape flips from `[speakerSlug][rosterId]` to `[day][rosterId]`; helper signatures `bmeGetAlertness(day, rosterId)` / `bmeSetAlertness(day, rosterId, rating)`. `bmeComputeStudentGrade` averages rated days (mean/3) instead of rated talks; new `perDay` field returned alongside `perTalk`. The Daily Attendance checklist's per-student row now renders three `.cb295-att-alert-btn` buttons (1/2/3) + a clear next to the present/absent checkbox; buttons are disabled when the student is marked explicitly absent. Per-student gradebook detail modal gains a new "Daily engagement" section (one row per day) and drops the per-talk alertness picker. Inline 22×10 alertness matrix removed from the gradebook panel. V3.281 migration prunes any pre-flip slug-keyed alertness entries.) — V3.280 (BME 295C admin gradebook. New `_classData.grades: { weights, alertness, discussionQuality, comments, excused }` field aggregates three signals: attendance (derived from `dailyAttendance` with explicit-present-for-grading semantics via `bmeIsPresentForGrade`, overriding the default-present semantics used for assignment), discussion participation (derived from `groupResponses.attendance.presentIds` gated by submitted status + group-quality stars; free-rider gate via `bmeStudentGotDiscussionCredit`), and alertness (manual 1–3 rating per student×talk). Composite uses normalized weights (default 40/30/30) — alertness weight redistributed to attendance + discussion when the student has no ratings yet (prevents "free points from not grading"). New gradebook dashboard card with sortable table + inline bulk-entry matrix + per-student detail modal (excused-day toggles, per-talk attendance/response/star/alertness pickers, comment textarea, autosave). CSV export for registrar. V3.280 migration seeds the field; persistAll trap extended. TEST_* rows filtered.) — V3.279 (Per-day attendance gating for group assignment. New `_classData.dailyAttendance: { [day]: { presentIds, absentIds, recordedAt, recordedBy } }` map written by a new Daily attendance dashboard card (per-day `<details>` with Tucson + Phoenix checklists, default-present checkboxes, Mark-all, Save, and Assign-{Day}-groups buttons). `autoAssignBmeSingleDay(day)` filters that campus's members to present students, scores against every OTHER day's existing assignments (so the re-roll keeps minimizing overlap), and clears `dailyGroups[day]` for absent students. `bmeIsPresentOnDay` defaults missing entries to present so the bulk all-days `autoAssignBme295cGroups` still works as before. V3.279 migration initializes the field; `persistAll` extended.) — V3.278 (Signed-in students light up the Roster & daily groups row. `signedIn` (existing per-row predicate derived from `_bmeDashboardData.profilesByEmail.has(roster[i].email.toLowerCase())`) now also stamps a `cb295-roster-row-signedin` class on the `<tr>`; CSS paints a green tint + inset left-edge accent. Header gains a `signedInCount/total signed in` chip when the dashboard cache has loaded. Test rows that also signed in keep amber background + green accent.) — V3.277 (Roster & daily groups admin table narrowed. Student ID / Email / Campus / High School collapsed into one `<details>` cell per row inside `renderBmeRosterMgmtPanel`; summary shows a campus chip (T blue / P amber / — grey) so the campus split is visible without expanding. Expand all / Collapse all toolbar buttons toggle every `<details>.cb295-roster-extras` at once. The same input classes (`.cb295-roster-email` / `.cb295-roster-campus` / `.cb295-roster-highschool`) move inside the details body, so the existing change handlers in `wireBme295cRoster` still fire on edits.) — V3.276 (Tucson parking cost note. `locations[].parkingNote` string renders as a small italic line under the parking buttons in both the syllabus location card and the schedule-tab top bar. Tucson's value is "Daily max: $9 at either garage."; Phoenix is unset so no extra line shows.) — V3.275 (Tucson gains Highland Garage as a second parking option. `locations[0].parkingOptions: [{ label, url }, …]` array supersedes the single `parkingUrl` field for the Tucson entry; render in both `renderBmeSyllabusBody` location card + `renderBmeScheduleBody` top bar now iterates the array when present, falling back to `parkingUrl` for the Phoenix entry that still uses the legacy single-link shape.) — V3.274 (Tucson room flipped to HSIB 880 for the whole week. `content.json.locations[0].room` is now `880` and the `roomNote` per-day-exception copy is removed; `renderBmeSyllabusBody`'s warning callout — already gated on `l.roomNote` truthiness — stays hidden without a code change.) — V3.273 (YouTube talk recordings on the Learning Modules tab. New `_classData.speakerVideos: { [slug]: 'youtube-url-or-id' }` map on the schedule doc, set per-speaker via a new **Talk videos** admin dashboard card. Render block in `renderBmeModulesBody` emits a 16:9 lazy-loaded `<iframe src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/{id}?rel=0&modestbranding=1" loading="lazy">` above the abstract when a video id is set; speakers without a value show no block. Helpers `bmeYoutubeIdFrom` (URL/id parser) + `bmeGetSpeakerVideoId` (cache reader); admin writer `setBmeSpeakerVideo` validates input + persists via `persistAll`. V3.273 migration initializes the field. YouTube absorbs all bandwidth — zero per-view cost regardless of how many students watch.) — V3.272 (Student-self-selected campus override. To let students change which campus they'll attend from without relaxing the schedule-doc admin-write rule, the bio modal writes a per-student override to `users/{uid}.bme295c.campus` (writable by the student via the existing owner-write rule). New helpers `bmeEffectiveCampus(entry, profile)` + `bmeProfileFor(entry)` route reads through the override; `bmeMatesForDay`, attendance-modal group filter, response-doc id construction, and the group banner header all pick it up. Admin views still read canonical `roster.campus` — admin re-runs auto-assign to integrate after students settle.) — V3.271 (Admin-controlled discussion gates + chat auto-tag. New `discussionState: { [speakerSlug]: 'pending'|'open'|'locked' }` map on `schedules/bme295c` is written by `setBmeDiscussionState` from a new Discussion Gates dashboard card + per-row Open/Lock toggle on the live monitor. `buildBmeResponseShell` + per-prompt input gating now require `state === 'open'` for edits; `pending` and `locked` render distinct read-only callouts. `bmeCurrentLiveSpeaker` prioritizes the first open speaker over the schedule-time derivation, so the Course Chat header + per-message `speakerSlug` auto-tag follow the admin's manual gate. Next-pending speaker gets an "Up next" blue accent on its module card. V3.271 migration seeds `discussionState: {}`; `persistAll` extended to include the field in its saveData payload (same trap V3.270 fixed for instructors/classAdminEmails/testMode).) — V3.270 (Schedule-save persistence fix. `persistAll()` was only including `tabs/sections/modules/studentRoster` in its Firestore payload — so admin-editable fields on `_classData.{instructors, classAdminEmails, testMode}` were written in memory but never reached the server. Because `ScheduleDB.saveSchedule` uses `set({ merge: true })` the server-side data stayed at its previous value, which surfaced as "I uploaded instructor photos + bios but they're gone after reload." Added field-presence guards to include each of those three fields in the saveData payload when present on `_classData`.) — V3.269 (Admin "Edit my profile" now routes to the Instructors panel on the admin dashboard instead of opening the student bio modal — that modal is shaped for student data (groups, mates, BME interest) and doesn't fit an admin. New button id `cb295-admin-edit-profile` in the admin check-in strip wires to a handler in `wireBme295cCheckin` that calls `switchTab(adminTab.id)` then rAF-defers a `scrollIntoView` on the V3.267 Instructors panel section (now carrying `id="cb295-instructors-panel"` as the scroll target).) — V3.268 (Live monitor table now lays out correctly. `.cb295-monitor-cell` had `display: flex` applied directly to `<td>` elements, which kicks them out of table flow in Chromium → all cells rendered stacked in one column. Moved the flex layout to a new `.cb295-monitor-cell-inner` child div; the `<td>` is now a normal table cell again, so the V3.266 flipped layout (rows = lectures, columns = T1..Tn P1..Pn) actually renders as a grid.) — V3.267 (Instructor profile photo + bio on the admin dashboard. V3.261 Instructors panel gains a left photo-column with preview frame + Upload/Replace photo button + a bio textarea spanning the field grid. Photo uploads write to `class-profile-photos/bme295c/{adminUid}/instructor-{idx}.{ext}` (storage.rules `request.auth.uid == userId` satisfied by the path's uid segment) then stamp `{photoUrl, photoStoragePath}` onto `_classData.instructors[idx]` and `persistAll()`. Bio textarea shares the `.cb295-instructor-field` class so it's picked up by the existing V3.261 Save loop. Reads added in three places: instructor info modal (photo + `.cb295-profile-view-bio` block), chat sidebar `instructorRow` (img when photoUrl set, initial chip otherwise), syllabus `instructorCards` (72×72 photo on top, bio below contact lines). All instructor identity now lives on the schedule's `instructors[]` array — single source of truth.) — V3.266 (Live monitor flipped to lectures × groups. Single unified table — rows = lectures in schedule order, columns = groups labeled `T1..Tn, P1..Pn` (Tucson blue chips, Phoenix amber, divider between the two campus runs). `bmeLiveMonitorInner` rebuilt to walk each campus's roster + daily group numbers and emit one column per group; renderCell unchanged.) — V3.265 (User-management page role-clobber fix. The website's `user-system.js` `loadUsers()` admin table uses a 4-role taxonomy (admin/editor/contributor/guest) but RM's admin-permissions page writes from a 10-role taxonomy (admin/pi/postdoc/phd/masters/undergrad/staff/collaborator/member/guest). When a user's role was a value the website dropdown didn't recognise, no `<option>` got `selected` and the next Save silently overwrote the stored role with the visible default. Two-part defensive fix: (1) row render prepends a sticky `Unknown: X — keep as is` option when `u.role` isn't in `ROLES`, so the dropdown's `.value` round-trips the stored role; (2) Save handler compares `roleSel.value` to the loaded `user.role` and skips the `role` write when unchanged. Belt-and-suspenders — closes the Cason-was-demoted-to-guest bug.) — V3.264 (BME 295C syllabus textbook-fee opt-out notice. New `content.feeNotice` block on `modules/bme295c/content.json` carries `title` + `body` + `steps[]` (steps can be plain strings or `{ label, href }` link objects). `renderBmeSyllabusBody` renders it as a `<section class="cb295-fee-notice">` amber callout at the very top of the syllabus card, above the overview key-facts. Block is optional — absent `feeNotice` removes the callout without code changes. First step links to `d2l.arizona.edu`; the rest are in-D2L navigation copy.) — V3.263 (BME 295C instructor email consolidated on `mcgheealex@gmail.com`. Admin signs in with the gmail account but `content.json` listed the instructor entry as `mcgheealex@arizona.edu`, so the chat members sidebar's `inst.email === viewerEmail` check failed and the admin wasn't recognized as themselves. `modules/bme295c/content.json` `instructors[0].email` flipped to gmail; three `mailto:` contact-instructor links in `class-builder.js` (check-in-no-roster strip, enroll-page hint, enroll-no-roster fallback) also retargeted to gmail. Sign-in identity now matches what the page displays.) — V3.262 (Prominent Map + Parking buttons. Syllabus location cards now render their map / parking / parking-PDF links as `.cb295-loc-link` button chips (blue for map, amber for parking) instead of inline text. New `cb295-locbar` block at the top of the Schedule tab mirrors the same buttons in a compact one-row-per-campus layout so students don't have to flip back to the syllabus the morning of a session.) — V3.261 (Admin moderation pack for BME 295C. `openBmeBioModal(opts)` + `saveBmeProfile(opts)` now accept an optional `targetUid` so admins can edit their own bio or any student's bio from one modal; `_bmeOtherProfileCache` value shape gains `_uid` so the admin-edit button knows which `users/{uid}` doc to target. Admin entry points: "Edit my profile" on the admin check-in strip + "Edit as admin" on the profile-view modal. Photo uploads still go through the owner's own auth.uid (Storage rule won't let admin upload as another user). Live-monitor cell modal grows a destructive "Delete response" so admin can clear stuck/test response docs without console access. Chat message bubbles now show author photos (denormalized `authorPhotoUrl` on the doc at send time so admins/non-roster authors get a real avatar without an extra Firestore read); anonymous messages stay emoji-only. Instructor info (`content.json.instructors`) becomes admin-editable via a new dashboard card; `bmeGetInstructors()` overlays `_classData.instructors` on top of `content.json` so syllabus/sidebar/info-modal all reflect the edits.) — V3.260 (Avatar photos render: chat sidebar `.cb295-chat-member-avatar` and group-banner `.cb295-mate-photo` both set `display: flex` on the base class for the initials chip, which silently collapsed any `<img>` element sharing the class. Added `img.<class> { display: block }` overrides so the actual bitmaps render at 28×28 / 34×34.) — V3.259 (Course Chat fixes: admin check-in strip no longer says "Groups not yet assigned" for admins-without-roster-entry; profile-view modal + sidebar avatars now render `<img>` photos correctly after splitting the broken `.cb295-profile-view-photo img` CSS that was applying `display:flex`; clicking a classmate triggers a fresh `users/{uid}.bme295c` fetch with lowercase+typed-case email fallback and triggers `refreshBmeChatMembersAside()` so the sidebar avatar updates in place. New `seedBmeChatCacheFromDashboard()` folds the dashboard's profile cache into `_bmeOtherProfileCache`.) — V3.258 (BME 295C chat-tab rename backfill — V3.257 renamed the seed + UI strings to "Course Chat" but legacy `schedules/bme295c` docs still carried the V3.251 string `"Live Q&A"` on their persisted `tabs[].name` and chat-section `name`. New migration in `wireClassPage` walks `schedule.tabs` immutably and rewrites any tab/section still named "Live Q&A" (or its HTML-encoded variant) to "Course Chat", then persists via `ScheduleDB.saveSchedule`. Idempotent — runs once per admin visit and is a no-op thereafter.) — V3.257 (BME 295C Tucson room override + chat tab rename. `modules/bme295c/content.json` Tucson location entry gains a `roomNote` field announcing Mon Jun 8 + Wed Jun 10 move to HSIB 880; `renderBmeSyllabusBody` in `class-builder.js` renders the note as an amber callout under the room line on the syllabus card (no-op when absent, so Phoenix is unchanged). Syllabus markdown's Locations bullet + Day 1 / Day 3 tables also call out the move. (2) Response model split — each speaker has 3 prompts; `groupResponses` doc shape switched from `text: string` to `prompts: { 0,1,2 }` with lazy normalization via `bmeNormalizeResponse` so legacy `text` docs still read. Surgical `rerenderBmeLiveMonitor()` swaps only `#cb295-live-monitor` innerHTML on each delta to preserve admin scroll. Click any cell → read-only modal with 3 prompts + named attendance (admin-only visibility — peer-facing response card hides attendance entirely).) — V3.251 (BME 295C Live Q&A chat. New always-on real-time chat scoped to `schedules/bme295c/classQA` subcollection. Two message types — raised-hand questions (🙋 Ask, admin can mark answered) and general chat (↗ Send). Optional anonymous toggle per post; anonymous messages display as "Anonymous" with the underlying `authorUid`/`authorName` still on the doc for admin moderation. Speaker auto-tagging via `bmeCurrentLiveSpeaker()` which maps today's day + current time to a `content.json` session slug. Clicking an author name (or admin's `(by RealName)` tag on anonymous messages) opens a read-only profile-view modal showing photo/bio/interest/campus/groups. Real-time updates via `onSnapshot`; subscription teardown wired into `cleanupListeners`. Firestore rule grants public read to signed-in users, create on `authorUid == auth.uid`, and update/delete to global admins + classAdminEmails. Test-mode wipe also clears tagged chat docs.) — V3.250 (BME 295C test mode + wipe. MAN_* and real students stay. Top-of-page red banner visible only to real admins (`_realIsAdmin && _classData.testMode === true`) keeps the state in sight. Entering test mode also idempotently injects 4 sample students if none exist. Real admins see a yellow strip above the tab bar with Admin / Student-picker / Public toggle that re-runs `wireClassPage` with a synthetic viewer identity (`_adminViewOverride`), letting them preview the student or public experience without signing out. New `tab_295c_admin` tab with `adminOnly: true` flag is filtered out of the tab bar for non-admins via a `buildTabBar` visibility predicate; section-level guard in `buildSectionBlockHTML` adds a second layer. The dashboard pulls user profiles in 10-email `where('email', 'in', ...)` chunks + all `groupResponses` docs (4 reads total for 22 students), cached in `_bmeDashboardData` for the page lifetime, and surfaces: class metadata grid + raw-JSON inspector, per-student status table (signed-in/bio/interest/photo/today's group/response count/last-edit) with per-row "View as" that jumps into student impersonation, and a latest-25 activity log derived from `users/{uid}.bme295c.updatedAt`, `groupResponses.lastEditedAt`, and `schedule.updatedAt`. V3.248 migration appends the new tab to legacy schedule docs that don't have it.) — V3.247 (BME 295C class admins. New `schedules/bme295c.classAdminEmails: string[]` field grants per-class admin rights without making someone a global admin. `wireClassPage` elevates `isAdmin = true` when the signed-in user's email is in the list (case-insensitive), and Firestore rules extend the schedule update/delete predicate to allow `request.auth.token.email in resource.data.get('classAdminEmails', [])`. A "Class admins" panel atop the admin roster section lets the course owner add/remove emails and copy a ready-to-paste invite message containing the class URL + the recipient's email. Class admins land on a friendlier check-in banner explaining they have admin powers + where to find them. Scope is intentionally narrow — class admins get global-admin-equivalent rights on this one schedule doc + its `groupResponses` subcollection only, with global-admin tooling (admin-permissions, RM, etc.) still gated by the `users/{uid}.role == 'admin'` check.) — V3.246 (BME 295C daily group rotation. Each roster entry now stores `dailyGroups: { Monday: n, ..., Friday: n }` per campus; the single `groupNumber` field is kept as a back-compat fallback (= Monday's group). Admin "Auto-assign daily groups" runs a per-day heuristic: 250-trial Fisher–Yates shuffle scored against a running pair-count map (`pairs[idA][idB] = days A and B already shared a group`); each day picks the partition with min total intra-group prior-pair score. With ~10 students/campus split into 3 groups, this typically brings repeat pairings to zero. Modules-tab banner shows today's group + a clickable 5-day rotation strip; per-speaker response box uses `bmeResponseDocId(campus, dailyGroups[speakerDay], speakerSlug)` so Mon-Group-#2 and Tue-Group-#2 (different cohorts under rotation) get separate Firestore docs. Bio modal + check-in greeting + enroll-success page all surface day-aware group info. Admin roster table replaces the single Group # input with 5 per-day inputs and gains "+ Add 4 test students" / "Clear test students" buttons (id prefix `TEST_`, amber tinting) for QA. V3.246 migration in `wireClassPage` backfills `dailyGroups` from legacy `groupNumber` across all 5 days when missing.) — V3.245 (BME 295C roster auto-populated. [class-builder.js](../../class-builder.js)'s `BME295C_ROSTER_SEED` now ships with `email`, `campus`, and `highSchool` filled in for every student — derived from `modules/bme295c/students.md` / `RPT_AD_ALL_NDS_APPS_BMEHSND-30355797.xlsx`. Two missing students (Kopp #24122754, Ung #24122574) added. Admin roster section gains a "High School" column; bio modal surfaces high school read-only beside campus/group. A V3.245 migration in `wireClassPage` backfills empty `email`/`campus`/`highSchool`/`pronouns` from the seed onto the persisted `schedules/bme295c.studentRoster` matched by Empl ID — admin overrides are preserved.) — V3.244 (BME 295C class page. Extends [class-builder.js](../../class-builder.js) with five class-specific `SECTION_REG` entries — `checkin295c`, `syllabus295c`, `schedule295c`, `modules295c`, `roster295c` — that power the three-tab page at `#/classes/bme295c`. Static course content (syllabus copy, speakers, abstracts, discussion points, photos) lives in-repo at [`modules/bme295c/content.json`](../../modules/bme295c/content.json) and `modules/bme295c/speaker-photos/`; dynamic state — admin-editable student roster with email/campus/group assignments — persists on `schedules/bme295c.studentRoster` via the existing public-read-admin-write rule. Student-owned profile fields (bio, BME interest, photo URL) live under a new `users/{uid}.bme295c` namespace on the existing user doc, writable only by the student per the existing user-doc rule. Profile-photo uploads use a new stricter storage path `class-profile-photos/{classId}/{userId}/...` with its own rule that enforces `request.auth.uid == userId`. Auth gate is by email-in-roster lookup, not new invitation tokens or roles — admin sets the expected email on each roster entry, student signs in with that email via Google OAuth and the page surfaces their preallocated info + group chip. Admin-only sections (`roster295c`) render to empty string for non-admin viewers via a guard in `buildSectionBlockHTML`. One-time migration in `wireClassPage` overlays pre-V3.244 schedule docs with the new layout while preserving any populated roster.) — V3.243 (Mentor assignments on the admin permissions page. Admin can designate one or more lab members as the **mentor** of another lab member via a new "Mentors" tab on [rm/pages/admin-permissions.html](../../rm/pages/admin-permissions.html); mentors then gain the same cross-user PMR access an admin has when viewing a student's PMR — read every block, post comments, assign tasks — without granting them blanket admin rights. New data field: `users/{menteeUid}.mentor_uids: string[]` on the existing user profile. New `firestore.rules` helper `isMentorOf(menteeUid)` does one `get()` on `users/{menteeUid}` and checks `request.auth.uid in data.mentor_uids` via `hasAny`; that helper is wired into the `userData/{userId}/pmr/{periodId}` read/write rule and the `userData/{userId}/pmr/{periodId}/comments/{commentId}` read/create rules alongside the existing `isAdmin()` / `isOwner()` / `researcher_uids` clauses. The PMR client ([rm/js/pmr.js](../../rm/js/pmr.js)) queries `users where mentor_uids array-contains <my uid>` once at boot for non-admins (single-field array-contains, no composite index) and populates `state.menteeUids`; the left rail now also renders for non-admin mentors filtered to self + their mentees only, with the heading "Mentees" instead of "Lab members". `switchToUser()` uses a new `canViewUid()` gate (self, admin, or in menteeUids) so mentors can click into any mentee's PMR. The existing `isReadOnlyView()` gate continues to apply — mentors can read + comment + assign tasks, but cannot edit the mentee's barriers, weekly entries, or projects, exactly mirroring admin cross-user behavior. Defense-in-depth note: the rules technically grant mentors write access (kept simple to match the `researcher_uids` precedent), but `isReadOnlyView()` short-circuits `scheduleSave` / `saveDoc` and disables contentEditable on every block when viewing someone else's PMR. Scope is PMR-only — mentors don't see the mentee's tasks queue, lab notebook, calendar, or any other personal data; the `userData/{userId}/{document=**}` wildcard still locks everything else down to owner-or-shared. Plan: [CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.243_mentor_assignments.md](../ClaudesPlan/V3.243_mentor_assignments.md). Previous: V3.242 — Training breakpoint standardization — Phase 3b housekeeping closeout of the RM mobile-compatibility plan. After V3.241 closed Phase 4, [rm/css/training.css](../../rm/css/training.css) was still carrying 10 non-canonical `@media (max-width: ...)` queries with 7 distinct breakpoints (420×2, 520, 600, 720×3, 820, 900×2) — leftover from before V3.232\x27s two-breakpoint discipline. V3.242 rounds all 10 to the canonical 640px (phone) and 1024px (tablet) values: `.scenario-bar` 420→640, `.panel` 900→1024, `.chart-row` 720→640, `.training-layout` 900→1024, `.arc-controls` 600→640, `.voice-grid` 720→640, `.triangle-layout` 820→1024, `.assessment-row` 520→640, plus the `.milestone-timeline` 3-tier waterfall (5 cols desktop → 2 cols tablet → 1 col phone) preserved by mapping its 720→1024 and 420→640 in sync with a comment explaining the intent so future passes don\x27t accidentally collapse the two-step waterfall into one rule. Most changes tighten the phone/tablet edge slightly with no visible regression; the one notable behavior change is `.milestone-timeline` on iPad portrait (1024px) which now shows 2 cols instead of 5 — acceptable trade-off for V3.233\x27s "tablet is a real target" theme. Other page-specific CSS files (chat 600/700/400; equipment 768; lab-notebook 720; meetings 700; procurement 700; scheduler 600; activity-tracker 700) still carry their own non-canonical breakpoints — the mobile plan is now in maintenance mode and each gets standardized when its page is rewritten, not on a separate cleanup pass. **The mobile/tablet compatibility master plan is now fully shipped.** Every Phase 1–4 item has landed: V3.232 design tokens, V3.233 shell adaptation, V3.235 side drawers, V3.236 page anchor, V3.237 calendar phone cards, V3.238 tasks inbox phone, V3.239 chat composer, V3.241 desktop-only interstitial, V3.242 training breakpoint cleanup. Plan: [CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.242_training_breakpoints.md](../ClaudesPlan/V3.242_training_breakpoints.md). `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 211 → 212.) ALSO V3.241 (Desktop-only interstitial — Phase 4 of the RM mobile-compatibility plan. Eleven RM pages fundamentally need a wide screen — multi-pane iframes ([rm/pages/split-view.html](../../rm/pages/split-view.html), [rm/pages/workflow-shell.html](../../rm/pages/workflow-shell.html)), admin matrices ([rm/pages/admin-migrate.html](../../rm/pages/admin-migrate.html), [rm/pages/admin-permissions.html](../../rm/pages/admin-permissions.html), [rm/pages/admin-tracker-migrate.html](../../rm/pages/admin-tracker-migrate.html)), financial dashboards with charts ([rm/pages/budget.html](../../rm/pages/budget.html), [rm/pages/finance.html](../../rm/pages/finance.html), [rm/pages/analytics.html](../../rm/pages/analytics.html)), grant + CV editors ([rm/pages/grants.html](../../rm/pages/grants.html), [rm/pages/cv-editor.html](../../rm/pages/cv-editor.html)), and the admin-only cross-user kanban ([rm/pages/lab-tasks.html](../../rm/pages/lab-tasks.html)). Forcing them into a phone viewport would degrade the desktop layout without producing a usable phone experience. V3.241 ships an opt-in interstitial: each adopter adds `class="rm-desktop-only"` to its `<body>` and loads a new self-contained IIFE [rm/js/desktop-only-guard.js](../../rm/js/desktop-only-guard.js) (~90 lines). At DOMContentLoaded the guard checks the body class + `window.innerWidth ≤ 640` + `sessionStorage rm_desktop_only_dismissed != '1'`; when all three hold it injects a `.rm-desktop-only-overlay` card with title, body, "← Back to RM home" link, and "Try anyway" button. "Try anyway" sets the sessionStorage flag and removes the overlay so the page underneath renders. Escape key acts the same as "Try anyway" (keyboard escape hatch). Focus lands on "Try anyway" so screen readers announce the dialog and tab-users can interact immediately. Resize-back-to-desktop tears the overlay down; resize-down-to-phone re-shows it unless dismissed. `window.DESKTOP_ONLY_GUARD` guards against double-init. Idempotent. Per-session dismissal (not localStorage) means a returning user sees the warning again on a fresh tab — the intent is to discourage phone use of tier-C pages, not permanently silence the warning. CSS counterpart in [rm/css/style.css](../../rm/css/style.css): `.rm-desktop-only-overlay` is `position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: var(--z-modal); background: rgba(15,23,42,0.92)` with `var(--safe-top)` / `var(--safe-bottom)` padding so notched phones don't clip the card; centered 360px-max-width `.rm-desktop-only-card`; primary + secondary `.rm-desktop-only-btn` styles hit `var(--tap-min)` 44px. The opt-in body class pattern beats URL-matching (keeps the desktop-only decision visible in each page's HTML, no central registry to maintain). Other admin / heavy pages (`email-review.html`, `chemicals.html` admin tabs) can opt in later by adding the body class + script tag. Desktop ≥641px is unaffected — the guard immediately returns when `window.innerWidth > 640`. **Phase 4 of the mobile-compatibility master plan is complete.** Phase 3b housekeeping (training.css breakpoint cleanup, 7 scattered values) remains as a future polish ship. Plan: [CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.241_desktop_only_interstitial.md](../ClaudesPlan/V3.241_desktop_only_interstitial.md). `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 210 → 211.) ALSO V3.240 (Paper Export Workspace gains compile history + staleness detection. V3.231 shipped the workspace with no memory: every compile produced a PDF, old ones lived in Firestore but the UI didn't surface them, and nothing told the author whether the current PDF matched the live draft. V3.240 adds (a) a **Compile history** panel in the left config column listing every compile for this paper with View + Delete buttons backed by an `onSnapshot` subscription to `paperExports/{paperId}/compiles` ordered by `triggeredAt` desc (limit 50), (b) **auto-load of the newest PDF** when the workspace opens via `firebase.storage().ref(compile.pdfStoragePath).getDownloadURL()` so the user lands on the most recent build without re-compiling (the 1-hour signed URL stored on the compile doc may have expired, hence the fresh mint), and (c) a **staleness banner** above the history panel — green "✓ Latest PDF matches the current draft" when fresh or amber "⚠ Draft has changed since the latest compile" (clickable to re-compile) when stale. Staleness compares a sha256 hash of every `.tex` + `.bib` file's `<relpath>\n<content>` joined `\n---FILE---\n` sorted by relpath; the Cloud Run service ([functions/paper-compile/server.js](../../functions/paper-compile/server.js)) computes the same hash via `crypto.createHash('sha256')` and persists it as `bundleHash` on the compile Firestore doc; the client recomputes via Web Crypto `crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', ...)` and compares — deterministic across both runtimes because both sides operate on the identical file representation. The banner also re-evaluates on every Yjs `update` event with 800ms debounce so authoring activity flips the banner amber immediately. Pre-V3.240 compiles in production lack `bundleHash` — the banner falls back to a neutral "Re-compile to enable change-detection" message rather than guessing. Delete plumbing: `firestore.rules` splits the prior blanket `allow write: if false` on `paperExports/{paperId}/compiles/{compileId}` into `allow create, update: if false` (still Admin-SDK-only, preserves the V3.231 invariant that only the Cloud Run service writes new compile records) and `allow delete: if isLabContributor()` so the UI's Delete button can land. `storage.rules` does the same on `paperExports/{paperId}/{allPaths=**}` — `create`/`update` still blocked, `delete` allowed for any signed-in user (the paired Firestore rule's `isLabContributor()` is the real gate, and `_deleteCompile` in [rm/js/paper-export.js](../../rm/js/paper-export.js) always deletes the Storage object first then the Firestore record so we don't orphan PDFs). When the currently-viewed compile is deleted, the snapshot listener auto-mounts the next newest one on its next tick. New paper-export state fields: `compiles`, `compilesUnsub`, `activeCompileId`, `localBundleHash`, `yjsObserveCleanup`. Helpers: `_compilesRef()`, `_subscribeCompiles()`, `_renderCompileList()`, `_formatWhen()` (relative-time formatter), `_viewCompile()`, `_deleteCompile()`, `_hashBundle()`, `_refreshStaleness()`, `_watchYjsForStaleness()`. New page section in [rm/pages/paper-export.html](../../rm/pages/paper-export.html) between "LaTeX add-ons" and "Figure defaults" with `#px-staleness` banner + `#px-compile-list` ul. New CSS: `.px-compile-list`, `.px-compile-list li.is-active` (blue tint on currently-mounted row), `.px-staleness.is-fresh` (green), `.px-staleness.is-stale` (amber, click-to-recompile cursor). Deploy: `firebase deploy --only firestore:rules,storage:rules` for the new delete clauses, redeploy Cloud Run for the `bundleHash` field on new compiles (`cd functions/paper-compile && gcloud run deploy paper-compile --source . --region us-central1 --port=8080 --no-allow-unauthenticated --memory 1Gi --timeout 90 --max-instances 3 --set-env-vars FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET=mcgheelab-f56cc.firebasestorage.app`), and Cyberduck-FTP the rm tree for the client. `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 208 → 209. Plan: `CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.240_paper_export_history.md`.) ALSO V3.239 (Chat phone composer keyboard fix — Phase 3b of the RM mobile-compatibility plan. The chat-layout at ≤700px (existing rule in [rm/css/chat.css](../../rm/css/chat.css)) lets `.chat-layout` flow into document scroll so the composer (`.chat-input-area`) sits in normal flex flow at the bottom of `.chat-main` — when iOS opened its keyboard the composer stayed at its original position, hidden behind the keyboard, and the user couldn't see what they were typing. V3.239 pins `.chat-input-area` to the viewport bottom via `position: fixed; bottom: 0; left: 0; right: 0; z-index: 20` at ≤640px. iOS Safari 15+ correctly tracks the visual viewport for `position: fixed` elements when the on-screen keyboard opens, so the composer rides up with the keyboard automatically — no `visualViewport` JS handler needed. `.chat-main { padding-bottom: 80px }` reserves space at the bottom of the messages list so the last message isn't hidden behind the floating composer. `.chat-input { min-height: 44px }` and `.chat-send-btn { min-width: 44px; min-height: 44px }` bring the composer and send button up to the 44px Apple HIG / WCAG 2.5.5 tap-target minimum (send button was 38×38). `padding-bottom: calc(.5rem + var(--safe-bottom))` on both `.chat-input-area` and `.chat-thread-input` clears the iOS home indicator on notched phones. The thread overlay (already `position: fixed; inset: 0` from the existing 700px block) keeps its own composer pinned at the bottom of the column-flex via the safe-area padding addition. The pre-iOS-15 `visualViewport.resize` + `translateY` JS pattern was considered but rejected: McGhee Lab's user base is on iOS 15+, and the CSS-only approach avoids a transient mis-positioning during keyboard transition. Real-device test recommended since Chrome DevTools doesn't faithfully simulate iOS keyboard behavior. Desktop ≥641px unchanged. Plan: [CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.239_chat_phone_composer.md](../ClaudesPlan/V3.239_chat_phone_composer.md). `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 208 → 209.) The `.inbox-row` rule (used by the Tasks inbox at [rm/pages/tasks.html](../../rm/pages/tasks.html)`?tab=inbox`, the Completed Tasks archive `?tab=archive`, and the cross-user [rm/pages/lab-tasks.html](../../rm/pages/lab-tasks.html) view) was the daily phone use case the user listed first ("Check my task inbox / mark done"). Desktop renders a flex-row with a 14px checkbox, ellipsis-truncated title, and meta chips; V3.233 hid the project/bucket crumb on phone to save horizontal space. V3.238 expands the existing 4-line `@media (max-width: 640px)` block in [rm/css/style.css](../../rm/css/style.css) into a 2-row `grid-template-areas` card: row 1 = checkbox + dot + main (title + crumb) + open-link; row 2 = meta chips left-aligned under main. Title drops `white-space: nowrap` and wraps to 2 lines. Checkbox grows from 14×14 to 20×20. Crumb restored to `display: block` (V3.233 had hidden it) since it answers "mark which task in which project?". Row padding bumps to `var(--sp-3)` so each row meets the 44px tap-target heuristic. `.inbox-open` gets `min-width: 28px; padding: var(--sp-1) var(--sp-2); font-size: 16px` for a real tap area. The `.inbox-row.completed-row` archive variant inherits the layout since it only adds border/title overrides on top. No JS changes — existing checkbox toggle + row-click handlers continue to work. Lab-notebook camera capture verified already-present (`<input type="file" id="ln-camera-input" accept="image/*" capture="environment">` at [rm/pages/lab-notebook.html:26](../../rm/pages/lab-notebook.html#L26)); no changes needed there. Desktop ≥641px byte-identical to V3.237. Plan: [CodeLog/ClaudesPlan/V3.238_tasks_inbox_phone.md](../ClaudesPlan/V3.238_tasks_inbox_phone.md). `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION 207 → 208.) ALSO V3.237 (Calendar / activity-row phone card layout — Phase 3b of the RM mobile-compatibility plan. The `.yr-line` (7-col: select / caret / date / main / meta / stars / actions) and `.ch-line` (6-col: date / cat / main / meta / stars / actions) activity rows used by [rm/pages/activity-overview.html](../../rm/pages/activity-overview.html), [rm/pages/calendar.html](../../rm/pages/calendar.html), and [rm/pages/email-review.html](../../rm/pages/email-review.html) were hardcoded to a desktop grid totaling ~680px minimum width — phones clipped them by ~280px before V3.237. New `@media (max-width: 640px)` blocks in [rm/css/style.css](../../rm/css/style.css) remap each row's children into a 2-row card via `grid-template-areas`: `.yr-line` collapses to a 3-col / 3-row layout (`var(--tap-min-compact) 1fr auto`) with select + main + actions on row 1, date + stars on row 2, meta on row 3; the caret column is hidden (the row-expand interaction is already tap-anywhere on touch). `.ch-line` collapses similarly: main + actions on row 1, date + meta + stars on row 2, category chip full-width on row 3. Both blocks drop `white-space: nowrap` on the title + sub inside `.col-main` so prose wraps to 2 lines instead of ellipsis-truncating — more useful than hiding info on a narrow screen. Same `.col-*` child elements as desktop, same row-click handler — only the grid template changes. Desktop ≥1024px byte-identical to V3.236. Page-specific CSS files (chat 600/700/400; equipment 768; lab-notebook 720; meetings 700; procurement 700; scheduler 600; activity-tracker 700; training 420/520/600/720×3/820/900) still carry their own non-canonical breakpoints — each will standardize as its page gets phone-attention in subsequent ships.
